```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
```
```
    }
}

```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class ArrayConcat {
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

+ You SHOULD output the code in its entirety, without omitting any original content.


assistant:
```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long)a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; \result[i] == a[i]);
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; \result[a.length + j] == b[j]);
      @   // original arrays are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // complete and disjoint behaviors
      @ ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; i <= j && j < m + n; ret[j] == ret[j]); // unchanged for other positions
```
```
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first a then b
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == a[k] : \result[k] == b[k - a.length]));
      @   // inputs are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness / consistency
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
```
```
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```
    //@  requires txt != null;

        int i = 0;
```

```
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class ArrayConcat {
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```
Note that:
+ You SHOULD generate `ensures` statements for every method, and `maintaining` statements for every loop.
+ Consider adding `/*@ spec_public @*/` specifications to all the members (especially private ones) within the class.

+ You SHOULD output the code in its entirety, without omitting any original content.

assistant:
```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long)a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; \result[i] == a[i]);
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; \result[a.length + j] == b[j]);
      @   // original arrays are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // complete and disjoint behaviors
      @ ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; i <= j && j < m + n; ret[j] == ret[j]); // unchanged for other positions
```
```
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
```
```
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
```
```
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
```
```
    }
}
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class ArrayConcat {
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;

      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long)a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; \result[i] == a[i]);
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; \result[a.length + j] == b[j]);
      @   // original arrays are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // complete and disjoint behaviors
      @ ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; i <= j && j < m + n; ret[j] == ret[j]); // unchanged for other positions
```
```
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            ret[i] = a[i];

          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
```
```
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/

            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
```

```
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing; // method does not modify any pre-existing heap locations
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first copy of a (using original values), then copy of b (using original values)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete
      @ ensures (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE) || (a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   // The method does not modify any pre-existing heap locations
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first the elements of a (in order), then the elements of b (in order),
      @   // all values taken from the original inputs
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // unified form: every position of result comes from either a or b as above
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete by construction of the preconditions above
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // newly allocated positions beyond i have no required relation yet
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == \old(b[j]));
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   // The method may only write into the newly allocated result array
      @   assigns \result[*];
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first copy of a (using original values), then copy of b (using original values)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // nothing else is stored in the result beyond these positions

      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {

            return null;

        int n = b.length;

          @   // the latter clause is a trivial placeholder saying other positions are unconstrained here
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
```

```
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:

      @   assigns \nothing;

      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
```
```
      @   // exact concatenation: first the elements of a (in order), then the elements of b (in order),
      @   // all values taken from the original inputs

      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));

      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));

      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete by construction of the preconditions above
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;

        int n = b.length;

          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // newly allocated positions beyond i have no required relation yet
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            ret[i] = a[i];
        }
```

```
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == a[k] : \result[k] == b[k - a.length]));
      @   // inputs are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness / consistency
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   // The method does not modify any pre-existing heap locations
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first the elements of a (in order), then the elements of b (in order),
      @   // all values taken from the original inputs
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // unified form: every position of result comes from either a or b as above
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete by construction of the preconditions above
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;

        int n = b.length;

          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // newly allocated positions beyond i have no required relation yet
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            ret[i] = a[i];
```

```
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first the elements of a (in order), then the elements of b (in order),
      @   // all values taken from the original inputs
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // unified form: every position of result comes from either a or b as above
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete by construction of the preconditions above
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // newly allocated positions beyond i have no required relation yet
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == \old(b[j]));
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            ret[i] = a[i];

          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
```
```
      @   // exact length
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first a then b
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == a[k] : \result[k] == b[k - a.length]));
      @   // inputs are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness / consistency
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
```

```
      @   assigns \result[*];
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first copy of a (using original values), then copy of b (using original values)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));

      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
```

```
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete by construction of the preconditions above
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // newly allocated positions beyond i have no required relation yet
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == \old(b[j]));
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class ArrayConcat {
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

assistant: ```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long)a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; \result[i] == a[i]);
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; \result[a.length + j] == b[j]);
      @   // original arrays are not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // complete and disjoint behaviors
      @ ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long)a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; i <= j && j < m + n; ret[j] == ret[j]); // unchanged for other positions
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);

          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
```
```
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
```
```
      @   assigns \nothing; // method does not modify any pre-existing heap locations
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first copy of a (using original values), then copy of b (using original values)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete
      @ ensures (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE) || (a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == a[j]);
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == b[j]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:

      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (no overflow due to precondition)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;

      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));

      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)

      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // completeness: the method returns null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @
```
```
      @ // behaviors are disjoint and complete by construction of the preconditions above
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;

        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // newly allocated positions beyond i have no required relation yet
          @ loop_writes ret[*];
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
```

```
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   // The implementation does not modify any pre-existing heap locations (it only allocates and fills a fresh array)
      @   assigns \nothing;

      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs

      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (precondition rules out overflow)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first the elements of a (in order), then the elements of b (in order),
      @   // values are taken from the original inputs (use \old to refer to pre-state)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // unified form for every result index (redundant but explicit)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // exact characterization: result is null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // positions >= i may be written later; this loop only writes into ret[0..m-1]
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == \old(b[j]));
          @ // this loop writes into ret[m .. m+n-1]
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Case when concatenation is not possible: return null
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @
      @ // Normal case: both arrays non-null and total length fits in int
      @ also
      @ behavior normalCase:
      @   requires a != null && b != null && (long) a.length + b.length <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
      @   // The implementation does not modify any pre-existing heap locations (it only allocates and fills a fresh array)
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   // result is a freshly allocated array distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of input lengths (precondition rules out overflow)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact concatenation: first the elements of a (in order), then the elements of b (in order),
      @   // values are taken from the original inputs (use \old to refer to pre-state)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < a.length; \result[k] == \old(a[k]));
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < b.length; \result[a.length + k] == \old(b[k]));
      @   // unified form for every result index (redundant but explicit)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents remain equal to their old values)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @
      @ // exact characterization: result is null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also
      @   ensures \result == null <==> (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE);
      @ @*/
    public static int[] arrayConcat(int[] a, int[] b) {
        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;
        
        int m = a.length;
        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // positions >= i may be written later; this loop only writes into ret[0..m-1]
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            ret[i] = a[i];
        }

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < m; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[m + j] == \old(b[j]));
          @ // this loop writes into ret[m .. m+n-1]
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases n - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            ret[i + m] = b[i];
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```
public class ArrayConcat {
    /*@
      @ // Two disjoint, complete behaviors:
      @ //  - nullCase: returns null when one input is null or total length would overflow int
      @ //  - normalCase: returns a fresh array that is the exact concatenation of a and b
      @
      @ behavior nullCase:
      @   requires a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE;

      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;

      @   // the method does not modify any pre-existing heap locations (it only allocates and fills a fresh array)
```

```
      @   // result is a freshly-allocated array, distinct from the inputs
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures \result != a && \result != b;
      @   // exact length = sum of the input lengths (precondition avoids overflow)
      @   ensures \result.length == a.length + b.length;
      @   // exact element-wise concatenation: result[0..a.length-1] == old(a[0..a.length-1])
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; \result[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   // result[a.length..a.length+b.length-1] == old(b[0..b.length-1])
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; \result[a.length + j] == \old(b[j]));
      @   // unified form (every result index is accounted for)
      @   ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < \result.length;
      @              (k < a.length ? \result[k] == \old(a[k]) : \result[k] == \old(b[k - a.length])));
      @   // inputs are not modified (their contents equal their pre-state)
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < a.length; a[i] == \old(a[i]));
      @   ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < b.length; b[j] == \old(b[j]));
      @   // permutation / multiplicity property: every value in result is from either a or b,
      @   // and the count of each value equals the sum of its counts in a and b
      @   ensures (\forall int v;
      @              (\num_of int p; 0 <= p && p < \result.length; \result[p] == v)

      @               + (\num_of int p; 0 <= p && p < b.length; \old(b[p]) == v));
      @
      @ // exact characterization: result is null iff the initial null/overflow condition holds
      @ also

      @ @*/

        if (a == null || b == null || (long) a.length + b.length > Integer.MAX_VALUE)
            return null;

        int n = b.length;
        int[] ret = new int[m + n];

        /*@
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i; ret[j] == \old(a[j]));
          @ // this loop writes only into the newly allocated array ret (positions 0..m-1)
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            ret[i] = a[i];

          @ decreases n - i;
```
